I benchmarked 1, 2, 3, 4 and 8 rows with 8 to 64 lanes; 2×32 was fastest.  73   Software prefetch gave no gain. Tails handle any `n` and `d`.  74 - **Attention:** walks the cache by position, reading each K/V row (all heads) contiguously. This   75   gave about 17% more speed at long context than the per-head strided walk. The head size is a  76   compile-time parameter for 16, 32, 64 and 128; a runtime-sized generic path covers any other size.  77   I tested that path by forcing it on, and it passes the check.  78 - **Vectorized element-wise steps:** rmsnorm, softmax, SwiGLU and the residual adds.  79 - **Summation order:** SIMD changes the order of float32 additions compared with the C scalar   80   loops. These are ordinary rounding-level differences; the measured error is about 2e-5.  81 - **RoPE:** the reference recomputes `cos`/`sin(pos*freq)` in every layer. Here they are computed  82   once per token and reused by all layers; the values are the same.  83   - The frequency constants follow `1/powf(10000, head_dim/(float)head_size)`. The exponent is  84     formed in float32 as in C.   85   - The power is evaluated in float64 and rounded to float32. Mojo's float32 `**` is  86     `exp(y*log(x))` in float32 and less accurate than libm `powf`. This affects only `head_size/2`  87     constants computed at load time. The main computation stays float32.  88 - **exp:** `exp` comes from Mojo's standard library (polynomial). It can differ from libm `expf`  89   by an ulp or so; `cos`/`sin` lower to the LLVM intrinsics.   90 - **stdin:** read through a raw file descriptor with my own line buffer.  91   `input()`/`_fdopen().readline()` open a new buffered `FILE*` on every call and could drop lines  92   when the harness pipelines commands.  93   94 ## Limitations / deviations   95   96 - `FIXED`/`LOGITS` with more than `seq_len` ids process only the first `seq_len` and report that  97   count (run.c cannot run past `seq_len`).  98 - Unknown commands get `ERR unknown command`; malformed numbers are parsed leniently.   99 - Weights are read with `read()` instead of `mmap`: about 0.4 GB of RAM, one-off load time before 100   `READY`. 101 - The public check only exercises this model (dim 768, 12 layers, 12 heads, head size 64,  102   seq_len 1024). Other shapes go through the generic code paths, which were checked only by forcing 103   the generic attention path on this model.╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌ Do you want to create NOTES.md?❯1.
